# Canned fruit cocktail

<section class="hmi-quick-read" id="canned-fruit-cocktail-quick-read" data-hmi-quick-read aria-labelledby="canned-fruit-cocktail-quick-read-label"><div class="hmi-quick-read-label" id="canned-fruit-cocktail-quick-read-label">Quick read</div>

Canned fruit cocktail in the HMI library has 43 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, tin, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn and Zn from 5 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms|5]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Trandafir I, Nour V, Ionica ME · 2012</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms">Determination of Tin in Canned Foods by Inductively Coupled Plasma-Mass Spectrometry</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Polish Journal of Environmental Studies</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<ul>
<li>
<strong>Metals measured:</strong>

Lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury and nickel and others, in 43 results from 5 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Main drivers:</strong>

Form, basis and where the canned fruit cocktail was sampled; reporting limits decide how many results are non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Practical framing:</strong>

Ask for lot results with a quantification limit well below the limit that applies in the destination market.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
</ul>

Canned fruit cocktail in the HMI library has 43 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, tin, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn and Zn from 5 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · href="../sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in canned fruit cocktail, as sold from 138 results in 4 reporting regions, with subgroup medians from 5.25 to 310 µg/kg.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> In the US Total Diet Study, canned fruit cocktail, canned had a median lead result of 5 µg/kg across 3 composites from 2018–2020.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Spungen et al. 2024 reported lead at 4.2 µg/kg (mean) in canned fruit cocktail, canned from all samples.<sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

For a buyer the practical points are the form bought, the basis a result is reported on, where the canned fruit cocktail came from and the laboratory’s reporting limit. Testing finds contamination but does not remove it.



<a id="ingredient-identity-and-scope"></a>
## What counts as canned fruit cocktail

<a id="hmi-ing-001"></a>
<a id="what-commodity-biological-species-edible-part-and-commercial-forms-does-this-page-cover"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-002"></a>
<a id="which-processing-states-and-derivatives-are-distinct-materials-rather-than-synonyms"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-003"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-can-be-transferred-between-those-materials-and-what-requires-a-separate-answer"></a>

This page covers canned fruit cocktail in 3 forms kept separate in every comparison: canned fruit cocktail, canned (25 results), canned fruit cocktail, as sold (17 results) and canned fruit cocktail, juice (1 result).<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms|5]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Trandafir I, Nour V, Ionica ME · 2012</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms">Determination of Tin in Canned Foods by Inductively Coupled Plasma-Mass Spectrometry</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Polish Journal of Environmental Studies</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/trandafir2012-tin-canned-foods-icp-ms">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Results stay on the basis each source reports: as sold or fresh weight, dry weight, or unstated. Dry-weight, as-sold and unstated results are never pooled, and no moisture conversion is applied.

## Heavy metal contamination profile of canned fruit cocktail

<a id="hmi-ing-007"></a>
<a id="which-metals-and-chemical-species-have-been-investigated-in-this-ingredient"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-008"></a>
<a id="what-concentrations-detection-frequencies-and-variability-were-observed-in-each-defined-sampling-frame"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-009"></a>
<a id="which-analyte-questions-are-unassessed-access-blocked-conflicting-or-searched-without-eligible-evidence"></a>

The table holds every extracted canned fruit cocktail result: one row for each statistic a source reports for a form, group and metal, 43 rows in all, grouped by metal and form; percentiles, ranges and maxima for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; 6 rows can be rated against an HMI reference for the same form and basis, 0 rows have a direct same-study comparison, and the rest stay unrated with the reason recorded. 2 values from 1 source failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.<sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<div class="hmi-analyte-explorer" id="hmi-occurrence-canned-fruit-cocktail" data-analyte-explorer data-compact-intro="true" data-source-summaries="true"><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-explorer-context><summary data-open-context aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="hmi-occurrence-canned-fruit-cocktail-drawer">Comparison methods and scope</summary><div data-explorer-context-content>

<p>Each row is one statistic a source reports for one form of canned fruit cocktail, group and metal; percentiles, ranges and maxima reported for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row. Different forms and preparation states are kept apart, and dry-weight results never share a reference with as-sold or fresh-weight results. Ratings place a result against an HMI reference built from monitoring results and ordinary-setting medians for the same form and basis. Same-study contrasts compare a contaminated or treated site with its control, targeted with random sampling, a prepared form with the unprocessed one, or one group of a study with the median of its other groups. Held rows failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.</p>

</div></details><p class="hmi-explorer-description" data-result-synopsis hidden><strong>First displayed analyte:</strong> <span data-result-synopsis-text>Lead: 16 findings from 4 sources. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>Alsabagh 2025 is the only compatible sampling family for canned fruit cocktail, canned on this basis in the completed canned fruit cocktail ledger, so this single-family reference cannot represent other markets. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>Limited data · HMI canned fruit cocktail Pb reference · Canned fruit cocktail, canned. median as reported: 5 µg/kg. HMI median 5; Q1 ≤4; Q3 5.5 µg/kg. 1 family. Too few reference units.</p><p>Only 3 reference unit is available; at least four are required by this descriptive display rule.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: Limited data. The position remains unresolved under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-fruit-cocktail-657d232989b4:f64d16dfee9c"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-fruit-cocktail-657d232989b4:f64d16dfee9c">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2018–2020</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>1 collection regions</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Purchase or reporting market; growing area not identified.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>As described by the monitoring programme.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p><p></p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. <a id="sources-and-contamination-pathways"></a>
## How metals reach canned fruit cocktail

<a id="hmi-ing-010"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-supports-root-uptake-surface-deposition-feed-transfer-or-other-biological-pathways"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-011"></a>
<a id="what-contamination-can-arise-from-agricultural-inputs-harvesting-processing-equipment-or-packaging"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-012"></a>
<a id="which-source-attribution-claims-are-measured-modelled-inferred-or-unresolved"></a>

No study in the library compared canned fruit cocktail from contaminated and ordinary settings, so the routes by which metals reach it are not measured here.



<a id="ranges-by-source-region-and-variety"></a>
## Which forms and regions measured lower

<a id="hmi-ing-013"></a>
<a id="how-do-concentrations-vary-by-origin-growing-conditions-variety-and-harvest-period"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-014"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-distinguishes-within-lot-between-lot-supplier-and-production-system-variation"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-015"></a>
<a id="which-comparisons-support-a-sourcing-decision-and-where-would-geographic-or-supplier-generalization-be-unjustified"></a>

The HMI reference for lead in canned fruit cocktail, as sold pools 34 subgroup units from 1 sampling family, with a median of between 9.25 and 11.3 µg/kg and a middle half spanning between 2.5 and 9 (lower quartile) and between 12.5 and 17 µg/kg (upper quartile).<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> For cadmium in canned fruit cocktail, canned, the reference median was below 1 µg/kg, with a middle half from below 1 to below 1 µg/kg across 4 units.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> For cadmium in canned fruit cocktail, as sold, the reference median was between 1 and 2 µg/kg, with a middle half from below 2 to between 1 and 10 µg/kg across 19 units.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Among GEMS/Food reporting regions, canned fruit cocktail, as sold had the lowest lead subgroup median in PAHO (5.25 µg/kg) and the highest in SEARO (310 µg/kg); reporting regions are markets, not growing areas.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<a id="mitigation-options"></a>
## Mitigation for canned fruit cocktail

<a id="hmi-ing-019"></a>
<a id="which-prevention-mitigation-or-remediation-measures-have-been-evaluated-for-this-ingredient"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-020"></a>

<a id="hmi-ing-021"></a>
<a id="what-evidence-supports-field-or-commercial-implementation-and-ongoing-verification"></a>

Lot testing against the applicable limit is the check that can be verified.

<p class="hmi-mitigation-gap" data-mitigation-gap>No measured way to lower metals in this food is in the library yet.</p>



<a id="trade-offs-and-failure-modes"></a>
## Trade-offs and limits of controls

<a id="hmi-ing-022"></a>
<a id="can-an-intervention-increase-another-contaminant-or-redistribute-contamination-to-a-by-product"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-023"></a>
<a id="what-effects-on-nutrition-yield-sensory-quality-stability-and-waste-streams-were-measured"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-024"></a>
<a id="what-costs-constraints-adverse-effects-or-failure-modes-are-documented-and-which-are-unknown"></a>

Metals do not move together, so a lot that passes a lead limit says little about cadmium, arsenic or nickel unless those are tested as well.



<a id="sampling-and-testing"></a>
## Testing canned fruit cocktail for metals

<a id="hmi-ing-025"></a>
<a id="what-sampling-design-is-appropriate-for-the-ingredient-heterogeneity-lot-and-decision"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-026"></a>

<a id="hmi-ing-027"></a>
<a id="what-can-a-certificate-of-analysis-or-non-detect-establish-and-what-remains-untested"></a>

Results depend on the method and its reporting limit. Reporting limits for lead ranged from 1 µg/kg (WHO GEMS/Food) to 3,000 µg/kg (WHO GEMS/Food), so a non-detect from a less sensitive method says much less.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a →</a></span></span></sup> Reporting limits for cadmium ranged from 1 µg/kg (FDA Total Diet Study) to 1,000 µg/kg (WHO GEMS/Food), so a non-detect from a less sensitive method says much less.<sup <a id="universal-faqs"></a>
## Canned fruit cocktail FAQs

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-canned-fruit-cocktail" data-anchor="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-canned-fruit-cocktail" data-question="What heavy metals are found in canned fruit cocktail?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>What heavy metals are found in canned fruit cocktail?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

The library reports lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, tin, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn and Zn in canned fruit cocktail, most often lead, cadmium and total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Alsabagh AAA · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Assessment of heavy metal and nitrate contamination in canned products from local markets in Baghdad</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Alnakhla Journal of Science</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in canned fruit cocktail, as sold from 138 results in 4 reporting regions, with subgroup medians from 5.25 to 310 µg/kg.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="which-regions-report-the-lowest-levels-in-canned-fruit-cocktail" data-anchor="which-regions-report-the-lowest-levels-in-canned-fruit-cocktail" data-question="Which regions report the lowest levels in canned fruit cocktail?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>Which regions report the lowest levels in canned fruit cocktail?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

Among GEMS/Food reporting regions, canned fruit cocktail, as sold had the lowest lead subgroup median in PAHO (5.25 µg/kg) and the highest in SEARO (310 µg/kg); reporting regions are markets, not growing areas.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="how-much-of-the-arsenic-in-canned-fruit-cocktail-is-inorganic" data-anchor="how-much-of-the-arsenic-in-canned-fruit-cocktail-is-inorganic" data-question="How much of the arsenic in canned fruit cocktail is inorganic?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>How much of the arsenic in canned fruit cocktail is inorganic?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

Inorganic arsenic, the more toxic form, was measured in 1 result from 1 source, far fewer than total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported inorganic arsenic in canned fruit cocktail, as sold from 4 results in 1 reporting region, with subgroup medians below 0.66 µg/kg, mostly non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>
